def calculate_premiums_C2() -> None:
    """4200-calculate_premiums
        DISPLAY "CALCULATING PREMIUMS..."
        SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
        PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
            READ insurance_master NEXT
                AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
                NOT AT END
                    PERFORM 4210-determine_base_premium
                    PERFORM 4220-apply_risk_factor
                    PERFORM 4230-calculate_final_premium
            
        
    """
    global ws_not_eof, ws_eof
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        # READ insurance_master NEXT
        # For simulation, using a placeholder
        if True:  # Replace with actual read condition
            ws_eof = True
        else:
            determine_base_premium_C2()
            apply_risk_factor_C2()
            calculate_final_premium_C2()

def determine_base_premium_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_calc_amount, insurance_master
    match True:
        case insurance_master.ins_life:
            ws_calc_amount = insurance_master.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * ws_life_rate_per_1000
        case insurance_master.ins_health:
            ws_calc_amount = ws_health_base_premium
        case insurance_master.ins_auto:
            ws_calc_amount = ws_auto_base_premium
        case insurance_master.ins_home:
            ws_calc_amount = insurance_master.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * ws_home_rate_per_1000
        case insurance_master.ins_umbrella:
            ws_calc_amount = ws_umbrella_rate
        case _:
            pass

def apply_risk_factor_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_calc_amount, insurance_master
    if insurance_master.ins_claims_count > 2:
        ws_calc_amount = ws_calc_amount * Decimal("1.25")

def calculate_final_premium_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_calc_amount, insurance_master, ws_total_premiums
    insurance_master.ins_premium_amount = ws_calc_amount
    ws_total_premiums += ws_calc_amount

def calculate_position_value_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global investment_master
    investment_master.inv_market_value = investment_master.inv_quantity * investment_master.inv_current_price

def calculate_gain_loss_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global investment_master
    investment_master.inv_gain_loss = investment_master.inv_market_value - (investment_master.inv_quantity * investment_master.inv_purchase_price)

def update_totals_C2() -> None:
    """5230-update_totals
        ADD inv_market_value TO ws_total_investments

    ws_total_investments += investment_master.inv_market_value

                compute_dividend_C2()
                post_dividend_C2()

def compute_dividend_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""

    ws_calc_amount = investment_master.inv_market_value * investment_master.inv_dividend_rate / 4

    report_line = ""  #MOVE SPACES TO report_line

def write_totals_C2() -> None:
    """6110-write_totals
        MOVE ws_total_deposits TO ws_formatted_amount
        STRING "TOTAL DEPOSITS: " DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_formatted_amount DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO report_line
        WRITE report_line
        
        MOVE ws_total_withdrawals TO ws_formatted_amount
        STRING "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_formatted_amount DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO report_line
        WRITE report_line
        
        MOVE ws_total_loans TO ws_formatted_amount
        STRING "TOTAL LOANS: " DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_formatted_amount DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO report_line
        WRITE report_line
    """
    global ws_formatted_amount, ws_total_deposits, report_line, ws_total_withdrawals, ws_total_loans
    ws_formatted_amount = str(ws_total_deposits)  # MOVE ws_total_deposits TO ws_formatted_amount
    report_line = "TOTAL DEPOSITS: " + ws_formatted_amount  # STRING
    print(report_line)  # WRITE report_line

    ws_formatted_amount = str(ws_total_withdrawals)  # MOVE ws_total_withdrawals TO ws_formatted_amount
    report_line = "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " + ws_formatted_amount  # STRING
    print(report_line)  # WRITE report_line

    ws_formatted_amount = str(ws_total_loans)  # MOVE ws_total_loans TO ws_formatted_amount
    report_line = "TOTAL LOANS: " + ws_formatted_amount  # STRING
    print(report_line)  # WRITE report_line

def format_date_C2() -> None:
    """8300-format_date
        STRING ws_temp_date(1:4) DELIMITED SIZE
               '-' DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_temp_date(5:2) DELIMITED SIZE
               '-' DELIMITED SIZE
               ws_temp_date(7:2) DELIMITED SIZE
               INTO ws_formatted_date
    """

def validate_account_C2() -> None:
    """8400-validate_account
        SET ws_valid TO TRUE
        IF acct_id  = None  # TODO: was SPACES

    ws_valid = True
    if acct_id == "":
        ws_invalid = True

def calculate_tax_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_calc_amount, ws_bracket_1_max, ws_bracket_1_rate, ws_calc_tax, ws_bracket_2_max, ws_bracket_2_rate, ws_bracket_3_max, ws_bracket_3_rate, ws_bracket_5_rate
    match True:
        case ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_1_max:
            ws_calc_tax = ws_calc_amount * ws_bracket_1_rate
        case ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_2_max:
            ws_calc_tax = (ws_bracket_1_max * ws_bracket_1_rate) + ((ws_calc_amount - ws_bracket_1_max) * ws_bracket_2_rate)
        case ws_calc_amount <= ws_bracket_3_max:
            ws_calc_tax = (ws_bracket_1_max * ws_bracket_1_rate) + ((ws_bracket_2_max - ws_bracket_1_max) * ws_bracket_2_rate) + ((ws_calc_amount - ws_bracket_2_max) * ws_bracket_3_rate)
        case _:
            ws_calc_tax = ws_calc_amount * ws_bracket_5_rate

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """9310-customer_segmentation."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    WS_EOF = False 
    while WS_NOT_EOF:
        try:
            CUSTOMER_MASTER = read_customer_master() # Assuming read_customer_master returns a CustomerMaster object
            calculate_clv_C3()
            assign_segment_C3()
        except StopIteration:
            WS_EOF = True
            WS_NOT_EOF = False

def read_customer_master() -> CustomerMaster:
    """Placeholder for reading a customer record."""
    # Replace this with actual data reading logic

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """9311-calculate_clv."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (CUSTOMER_MASTER.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE * WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + \
                       (CUSTOMER_MASTER.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS * WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + \
                       (CUSTOMER_MASTER.CUST_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS * Decimal("0.01"))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """9312-assign_segment."""
    global WS_TEMP_CODE, WS_CALC_RESULT
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

def sweep_accounts_C3() -> None:
    """9632-sweep_accounts."""
    global ACCT, WS_CALC_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    if ACCT.ACCT_BALANCE > ACCT.ACCT_MIN_BALANCE:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ACCT.ACCT_BALANCE - ACCT.ACCT_MIN_BALANCE
        ACCT.ACCT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

    tran_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")  # PIC 9(8)V99

ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

def c000_aml_extended_C4() -> None:
    """C000-aml_extended."""COBOL logic"""C100-transaction_monitoring."""
    global ws_not_eof, ws_eof, transaction_log  # added global keyword
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")  # DISPLAY "MONITORING TRANSACTIONS..."
    ws_not_eof = True  # SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
    while ws_not_eof:  # PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
        #READ transaction_log NEXT
        #Assuming transaction log is a list of TransactionLog objects
        try:
            transaction_log = next(transaction_log_iterator)
            c110_rule_based_detection_C4()  # PERFORM C110-rule_based_detection
            c120_behavior_analysis_C4()  # PERFORM C120-behavior_analysis
            c130_network_analysis_C4()  # PERFORM C130-network_analysis
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof = True #SET ws_eof TO TRUE
            ws_not_eof = False
        #
    #

def main_loop() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WORK_AREAS
    WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_EOF:
        try:
            # Assuming a read function that simulates reading the next record
            customer_master = read_customer_master_next()
            i110_update_profile_C5()
            i120_enrich_profile_C5()
            WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_CUST_COUNT += 1
        except StopIteration:
            WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_EOF = True

def read_customer_master_next():
  """ Dummy function to simulate reading customer_master"""
  yield CUSTOMER_MASTER
  raise StopIteration

def main_control_0000_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    initialization_1000_C5()
    while WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        process_transactions_2000_C5()
    finalization_9000_C5()

def initialization_1000_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WORK_AREAS, REPORT_HEADER
    WS_WORK_AREAS = WorkAreas()
    WS_COUNTERS = Counters()
    WS_TOTALS = Totals()
    WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_CURRENT_DATETIME = str(datetime.now())[:10]  # Simplified current_date
    REPORT_HEADER.RPT_YEAR = WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_CURR_YEAR = str(datetime.now().year)
    REPORT_HEADER.RPT_MONTH = WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_CURR_MONTH = str(datetime.now().month).zfill(2)
    REPORT_HEADER.RPT_DAY = WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_CURR_DAY = str(datetime.now().day).zfill(2)
    open_files_1100_C5()
    read_parameters_1200_C5()
    initialize_tables_1300_C5()
    load_reference_data_1400_C5()

def open_files_1100_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WORK_AREAS
    # Simulate file opening - in real code, handle exceptions
    try:
      customer_file = open(CUSTOMER_FILE, 'r')
      account_file = open(ACCOUNT_FILE, 'r')
      transaction_file = open(TRANSACTION_FILE, 'r')
      report_file = open(REPORT_FILE, 'w')
      error_file = open(ERROR_FILE, 'w')
      master_file = open(MASTER_FILE, 'r+') # I-O mode
      WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_FILE_STATUS = '00'
    except Exception as e:
        WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_FILE_STATUS = '99' # Assume '99' is file error
        WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'
        abort_process_9500_C5()

def read_parameters_1200_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WORK_AREAS
    today = datetime.now()
    WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_PARAM_DATE = today.strftime('%Y%m%d')
    WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_PARAM_TIME = today.strftime('%H%M%S')
    WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_JOB_ID = 'batch_001'
    WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_ENV_TYPE = 'PRODUCTION'
    WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_PROCESS_DATE = int(today.strftime('%Y%m%d'))

def initialize_tables_1300_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WORK_AREAS
    rate_table = [RateTableEntry() for _ in range(100)] # Pythonic table
    branch_table = [BranchTableEntry() for _ in range(50)] # Pythonic table
    for ws_tbl_idx in range(1, 101):
      rate_table_entry = RateTableEntry()  # init like COBOL
      rate_table_entry.RT_RATE = Decimal("0.00")
      rate_table_entry.RT_CODE = " " * len(rate_table_entry.RT_CODE) # blank fill

    for ws_tbl_idx in range(1, 51):
      branch_table_entry = BranchTableEntry() # init like COBOL

def load_reference_data_1400_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WORK_AREAS, REFERENCE_RECORD, RATE_TABLE_ENTRY
    WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_TBL_IDX = 1
    WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    rate_table = [RateTableEntry() for _ in range(100)]

    try:
        with open(REFERENCE_FILE, 'r') as ref_file:
            for line in ref_file:
                if WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y' or WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_TBL_IDX > 100:
                    break

                parts = line.strip().split(',')
                if len(parts) == 2:
                  WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_CODE = parts[0]
                  WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_RATE = Decimal(parts[1])

                  rate_table_entry = RateTableEntry()
                  rate_table_entry.RT_CODE = WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_CODE
                  rate_table_entry.RT_RATE = WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_REF_RECORD.WS_REF_RATE
                  rate_table[WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_TBL_idx_1] = rate_table_entry
                  WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_TBL_IDX += 1
                else:
                  print(f"Skipping invalid line: {line}")

    except FileNotFoundError:
        WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y' # act like AT END

    WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def process_transactions_2000_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WORK_AREAS, TRANSACTION_RECORD
    try:
        with open(TRANSACTION_FILE, 'r') as transaction_file:
            line = next(transaction_file)
            parts = line.strip().split(',')
            if len(parts) == 4:
              WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID = parts[0]
              WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT = Decimal(parts[1])
              WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE = parts[2]
              WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TARGET_ACCOUNT = parts[3]
              WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_TRANS_COUNT += 1
              validate_transaction_2100_C5()
              if WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_VALID_FLAG == 'Y':
                  process_by_type_2200_C5()
              else:
                  handle_error_2900_C5()
            else:
              print(f"Skipping invalid transaction line: {line}")

    except FileNotFoundError:
        WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

    except StopIteration: # File END
        WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def validate_transaction_2100_C5() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WORK_AREAS, TRANSACTION_RECORD
    WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'Y'
    if not WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_ACCOUNT_ID.strip():
        WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
        return

    try:
        Decimal(WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_AMOUNT)
    except:
        WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_VALID_FLAG = 'N'
        WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        return

    txn_type = WS_WORK_AREAS.WS_TRANSACTION_REC.TXN_TYPE
    if txn_type not in ('D', 'W', 'T', ''

WS_INTEREST_RATE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')

def inline_evaluate_C6(value: str) -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    WHEN OTHER
       MOVE 2.5 TO ws_interest_rate
    
    match value:
        case _:
            global WS_INTEREST_RATE
            WS_INTEREST_RATE = Decimal('2.5')

def calculate_simple_interest_C6() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST
    WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE * WS_INTEREST_RATE * Decimal(WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD) / Decimal('36500.00')

def calculate_compound_interest_C6() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR, WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST
    WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR = (1 + WS_INTEREST_RATE / Decimal('36500')) ** WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD
    WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE * (WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR - 1)

def apply_interest_C6() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    if WS_INTEREST_METHOD == 'S':
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST
    else:
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST
    update_account_2350_C6()

def fee_processing_8000_C6() -> None:
    # COBOL reference preserved
    PERFORM 8200-calculate_transaction_fees
    PERFORM 8300-apply_fee_waivers
    PERFORM 8400-deduct_fees."""
    calculate_monthly_fee_8100_C6()
    calculate_transaction_fees_8200_C6()
    apply_fee_waivers_8300_C6()
    deduct_fees_8400_C6()

def calculate_monthly_fee_8100_C6() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""IF ws_trans_count > ws_free_trans_limit
       COMPUTE ws_excess_trans = 
          ws_trans_count - ws_free_trans_limit
       COMPUTE ws_trans_fee = 
          ws_excess_trans * ws_per_trans_fee

       MOVE ZEROES TO ws_trans_fee
    
    global WS_EXCESS_TRANS, WS_TRANS_FEE
    if WS_TRANS_COUNT > WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT:
        WS_EXCESS_TRANS = WS_TRANS_COUNT - WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT
        WS_TRANS_FEE = Decimal(WS_EXCESS_TRANS) * WS_PER_TRANS_FEE
    else:
        WS_TRANS_FEE = Decimal('0.00')

def apply_fee_waivers_8300_C6() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""COMPUTE ws_total_fees = 
       ws_monthly_fee + ws_trans_fee
    SUBTRACT ws_total_fees FROM ws_account_balance
    PERFORM 2350-update_account
    PERFORM 8450-record_fee_transaction."""
    global WS_TOTAL_FEES, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    WS_TOTAL_FEES = WS_MONTHLY_FEE + WS_TRANS_FEE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS

    trade_rec_time: int = 0

def calculate_pmi_C7(ws_ltv_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """10335-calculate_pmi
    """
    if ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
    else:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0050') / 12
    return ws_pmi_amount

def evaluate_history_C7(ws_late_90_days: int, ws_late_60_days: int, ws_late_30_days: int, ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str, str, str]:
    """10340-evaluate_history
    """
    ws_factor_1: str = ""
    ws_factor_2: str = ""
    ws_factor_3: str = ""

    if ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws_risk_score -= 50
        ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws_risk_score -= 30
        ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws_risk_score -= 20
        ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'

    return ws_risk_score, ws_factor_1, ws_factor_2, ws_factor_3

def calculate_final_risk_C7(ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """10350-calculate_final_risk
    """
    ws_risk_score = ws_risk_score // 4
    ws_risk_category: str = ""
    match True:
        case ws_risk_score >= 80:
            ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
        case ws_risk_score >= 60:
            ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
        case ws_risk_score >= 40:
            ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
        case _:
            ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_risk_category

def determine_approval_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str, ws_dti_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal]:
    """10400-determine_approval
    """
    ws_approval_status: str = ""
    ws_conditions: str = ""
    ws_approved_amount: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    ws_approved_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')

    if ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate

    ws_approval_status = 'A'
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    ws_approved_rate = calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_credit_tier, ws_base_rate, ws_risk_category)

    return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate

def calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_base_rate: Decimal, ws_risk_category: str) -> Decimal:
    """10450-calculate_approved_terms
    """
    ws_approved_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')

    match ws_credit_tier:
        case 'A':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.00')
        case 'B':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.50')
        case 'C':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('1.50')
        case 'D':
            ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('3.00')

    if ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        ws_approved_rate += Decimal('0.50')

    return ws_approved_rate

def generate_loan_terms_C7(ws_approved_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """10500-generate_loan_terms
    """
    ws_loan_interest_rate: Decimal = ws_approved_rate
    ws_monthly_rate: Decimal = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    ws_compound_factor: Decimal = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    ws_loan_principal_bal: Decimal = ws_loan_amount

    return ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_monthly_rate, ws_compound_factor, ws_loan_monthly_pmt

def create_amortization_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, loan_mortgage: LoanMortgage, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal):
    """10600-create_amortization
    """
    global WS_RUNNING_BALANCE, WS_PAYMENT_DATE, WS_AMORT_IDX, AMORT_INTEREST, AMORT_PRINCIPAL, AMORT_BALANCE, AMORT_PAYMENT_NUM, AMORT_PAYMENT_AMT, AMORT_ESCROW, AMORT_TOTAL_PMT
    WS_RUNNING_BALANCE = ws_loan_amount
    WS_PAYMENT_DATE = int(datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"))  # Simulate current_date

    for WS_AMORT_IDX in range(1, ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        amort_interest_val, amort_principal_val, amort_balance_val, amort_payment_num_val, amort_payment_amt_val, amort_escrow_val, amort_total_pmt_val = calculate_payment_split_C7(WS_RUNNING_BALANCE, WS_MONTHLY_RATE, WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT, loan_mortgage, ws_pmi_amount, WS_AMORT_IDX)

        AMORT_INTEREST[WS_AMORT_idx_1] = amort_interest_val
        AMORT_PRINCIPAL[WS_AMORT_idx_1] = amort_principal_val
        AMORT_BALANCE[WS_AMORT_idx_1] = amort_balance_val
        AMORT_PAYMENT_NUM[WS_AMORT_idx_1] = amort_payment_num_val
        AMORT_PAYMENT_AMT[WS_AMORT_idx_1] = amort_payment_amt_val
        AMORT_ESCROW[WS_AMORT_idx_1] = amort_escrow_val
        AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[WS_AMORT_idx_1] = amort_total_pmt_val

def calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance: Decimal, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, loan_mortgage: LoanMortgage, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal, ws_amort_idx: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, int, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """10650-calculate_payment_split
    """
    global WS_PAYMENT_MONTH, WS_PAYMENT_YEAR, AMORT_INTEREST, AMORT_PRINCIPAL, AMORT_BALANCE, AMORT_PAYMENT_NUM, AMORT_PAYMENT_AMT, AMORT_ESCROW, AMORT_TOTAL_PMT, WS_RUNNING_BALANCE

    amort_interest: Decimal = ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate
    amort_principal: Decimal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest
    ws_running_balance -= amort_principal
    amort_balance: Decimal = ws_running_balance
    amort_payment_num: int = ws_amort_idx
    amort_payment_amt: Decimal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt

    if loan_mortgage.loan_mortgage:
        amort_escrow: Decimal = (loan_mortgage.ws_property_tax + loan_mortgage.ws_insurance_premium) / 12
        amort_total_pmt: Decimal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow + ws_pmi_amount
    else:
        amort_total_pmt: Decimal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt

    ws_payment_month_val, ws_payment_year_val, amort_payment_date_val = advance_payment_date_C7(ws_amort_idx)
    WS_PAYMENT_MONTH = ws_payment_month_val
    WS_PAYMENT_YEAR = ws_payment_year_val

    return amort_interest, amort_principal, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_escrow, amort_total_pmt

def advance_payment_date_C7(ws_amort_idx: int) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    """10660-advance_payment_date
    """
    global WS_PAYMENT_MONTH, WS_PAYMENT_YEAR, AMORT_PAYMENT_DATE

    ws_payment_month: int = WS_PAYMENT_MONTH + 1
    ws_payment_year: int  = None  # TODO: was WS_PAYMENT_YEAR

    if ws_payment_month > 12:
        ws_payment_month = 1
        ws_payment_year += 1

    amort_payment_date: int = ws_payment_year * 10000 + ws_payment_month * 100 + 1

    return ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year, amort_payment_date

def finalize_loan_C7(ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_id: str, ws_loan_type: str, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_interest_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal):
    """10700-finalize_loan
    """
    global WS_LOAN_START_DATE, WS_LOAN_END_DATE, WS_LOAN_STATUS
    WS_LOAN_START_DATE = int(datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d"))  # Simulate current_date
    WS_LOAN_END_DATE = WS_LOAN_START_DATE + (ws_loan_term_months * 30)
    WS_LOAN_STATUS = 'A'
    create_loan_record_C7(ws_loan_id, ws_loan_type, ws_loan_amount, ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, WS_LOAN_START_DATE, WS_LOAN_STATUS)
    disburse_funds_C7(ws_loan_amount)
    send_confirmation_C7()

def create_loan_record_C7(ws_loan_id: str, ws_loan_type: str, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_interest_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, ws_loan_start_date: int, ws_loan_status: str):
    """10750-create_loan_record

    global WS_LOAN_RECORD

    WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_type = ws_loan_type
    WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_amount = ws_loan_amount
    WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate
    WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_payment = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_start = ws_loan_start_date
    WS_LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_status = ws_loan_status
    write_loan_record(WS_LOAN_RECORD)  # Assuming this writes to a file or database

def portfolio_management_C7():
    """11000-portfolio_management
    """
    load_portfolio_C7()
    update_market_prices_C7()
    calculate_values_C7()
    rebalance_check_C7()
    generate_statements_C7()

def load_portfolio_C7():
    """11100-load_portfolio
    """
    global WS_HOLD_IDX, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_HOLDING_REC, WS_HOLDING, WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT
    WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
    WS_EOF_FLAG = ''
    while WS_HOLD_IDX <= 100 and WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        holding_rec, ws_eof_flag_val = read_holdings_file()
        WS_EOF_FLAG = ws_eof_flag_val
        if WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            WS_HOLDING_REC = holding_rec
            WS_HOLDING[WS_HOLD_idx_1]  = None  # TODO: was WS_HOLDING_REC
            WS_HOLD_IDX += 1
    WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT = WS_HOLD_IDX - 1

def update_market_prices_C7():
    """11200-update_market_prices
    """
    global WS_HOLD_IDX
    for WS_HOLD_IDX in range(1, WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT + 1):
        ws_quote_symbol_val = HOLD_SYMBOL[WS_HOLD_idx_1]
        WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL = ws_quote_symbol_val
        ws_quote_price_val = get_quote_C7(WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL)
        HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE[WS_HOLD_idx_1] = ws_quote_price_val

def get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol: str) -> Decimal:
    """11250-get_quote
    """
    global WS_QUOTE_PRICE
    quote_request: QuoteRequest = QuoteRequest(quote_request_symbol = ws_quote_symbol)
    quote_response: QuoteResponse = call_getquote(quote_request)
    if quote_response.quote_response_status == 'OK':
        WS_QUOTE_PRICE = quote_response.quote_last_price
    else:
        WS_QUOTE_PRICE = Decimal('0.00')
    return WS_QUOTE_PRICE

def calculate_values_C7():
    """11300-calculate_values
    """
    global WS_TOTAL_VALUE, WS_COST_BASIS, WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN, WS_HOLD_IDX
    WS_TOTAL_VALUE = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_COST_BASIS = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN = Decimal('0.00')
    for WS_HOLD_IDX in range(1, WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT + 1):
        calculate_holding_value_C7()

def calculate_holding_value_C7():
    """11350-calculate_holding_value
    """
    global HOLD_MARKET_VALUE, WS_HOLD_COST, HOLD_GAIN_LOSS, HOLD_PCT_CHANGE, WS_TOTAL_VALUE, WS_COST_BASIS, WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN, WS_HOLD_IDX
    HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[WS_HOLD_idx_1] = Decimal(HOLD_SHARES[WS_HOLD_idx_1]) * HOLD_CURRENT_PRICE[WS_HOLD_idx_1]
    WS_HOLD_COST = Decimal(HOLD_SHARES[WS_HOLD_idx_1]) * HOLD_COST_PER_SHARE[WS_HOLD_idx_1]
    HOLD_GAIN_LOSS[WS_HOLD_idx_1] = HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[WS_HOLD_idx_1] - WS_HOLD_COST
    if WS_HOLD_COST > 0:
        HOLD_PCT_CHANGE[WS_HOLD_idx_1] = (HOLD_GAIN_LOSS[WS_HOLD_idx_1] / WS_HOLD_COST) * 100
    else:
        HOLD_PCT_CHANGE[WS_HOLD_idx_1] = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_TOTAL_VALUE += HOLD_MARKET_VALUE[WS_HOLD_idx_1]
    WS_COST_BASIS += None  # TODO: was WS_HOLD_COST
    WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN += HOLD_GAIN_LOSS[WS_HOLD_idx_1]

    calculate_current_allocation_C7()
    compare_to_target_C7()
    if WS_REBALANCE_NEEDED == 'Y':
        generate_rebalance_trades_C7()

def calculate_premium_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13220-calc_auto_premium
    """
    match global_data.WS_DRIVER_AGE:
        case age if 16 <= age <= 25:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("500.00")
        case age if 26 <= age <= 50:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("300.00")
        case age if 51 <= age <= 75:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("400.00")
        case _:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("600.00")

    match global_data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR:
        case 1:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal("50.00")
        case acc if 2 <= acc <= 5:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal("100.00")
        case _:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal("50.00")

    match global_data.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR:
        case vio if 6 <= vio <= 10:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal("100.00")
        case _:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += Decimal("50.00")

    if global_data.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 25:
        global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")

    if global_data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 0:
        global_data.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE = global_data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR * Decimal("200.00")
        global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += global_data.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE

    if global_data.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR > 0:
        global_data.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE = global_data.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR * Decimal("100.00")
        global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += global_data.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE

    global_data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    global_data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = global_data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal("12.0")

def calc_home_premium_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13230-calc_home_premium
    """
    global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = global_data.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.003")
    match global_data.WS_HOME_AGE:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 10:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")
        case age if 11 <= age <= 25:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
        case age if 26 <= age <= 50:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.2")
        case _:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")

    if global_data.WS_FLOOD_ZONE == 'Y':
        global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")

    if global_data.WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM == 'Y':
        global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")

    global_data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT = (global_data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE / Decimal("1000.0")) * Decimal("50.0")
    global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM -= global_data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT

    if global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM < Decimal("200.0"):
        global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("200.0")

    global_data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    global_data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = global_data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal("12.0")

def calc_health_premium_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13240-calc_health_premium
    """
    global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal("300.0")
    match global_data.WS_INSURED_AGE:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 18:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.5")
        case age if 19 <= age <= 30:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
        case age if 31 <= age <= 40:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3")
        case age if 41 <= age <= 50:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6")
        case age if 51 <= age <= 60:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.0")
        case _:
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.8")

    match global_data.WS_PLAN_TYPE:
        case 'BRONZE':
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.8")
        case 'SILVER':
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.0")
        case 'GOLD':
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.3")
        case 'PLATINUM':
            global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.6")

    if global_data.WS_FAMILY_PLAN == 'Y':
        global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal("2.5")

    global_data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = global_data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    global_data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = global_data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM * Decimal("12.0")

def underwriting_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13300-UNDERWRITING
    """
    evaluate_risk_factors_C8(global_data)
    check_medical_history_C8(global_data)
    verify_information_C8(global_data)
    determine_decision_C8(global_data)

def evaluate_risk_factors_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13310-evaluate_risk_factors
    """
    global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS = 0
    if global_data.POLICY_LIFE:
        if global_data.WS_BMI > 30:
            global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
        if global_data.WS_SMOKER_FLAG == 'Y':
            global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 25
        if global_data.WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION == 'Y':
            global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15
    if global_data.POLICY_AUTO:
        if global_data.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 21:
            global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        if global_data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 1:
            global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15

def check_medical_history_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13320-check_medical_history
    """
    if global_data.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS > 0:
        global_data.WS_CONDITION_POINTS = global_data.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS * 5
        global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS += global_data.WS_CONDITION_POINTS
    if global_data.WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION == 'Y':
        global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
    if global_data.WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT > 5:
        global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 5

def verify_information_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13330-verify_information
    """
    check_fraud_indicators_C8(global_data)
    validate_documents_C8(global_data)

def check_fraud_indicators_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13335-check_fraud_indicators
    """
    if global_data.WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 3:
        global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        global_data.WS_FRAUD_FLAG = 'Y'
    if global_data.WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH == 'Y':
        global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10

def validate_documents_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13336-validate_documents
    """
    if global_data.WS_DOC_MISSING == 'Y':
        global_data.WS_UW_STATUS = 'PENDING'
    else:
        global_data.WS_UW_STATUS = 'COMPLETE'

def determine_decision_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13340-determine_decision
    """
    match True:
        case bool(global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 50):
            global_data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'DECLINE'
        case bool(global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 30):
            global_data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'SUBSTANDARD'
            global_data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("1.5")
        case bool(global_data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 15):
            global_data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'STANDARD'
        case _:
            global_data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'PREFERRED'
            global_data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal("0.9")

def issue_policy_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13400-issue_policy
    """
    if global_data.WS_UW_DECISION != 'DECLINE':
        generate_policy_number_C8(global_data)
        create_policy_record_C8(global_data)
        set_beneficiaries_C8(global_data)
        send_policy_docs_C8(global_data)
    else:
        send_decline_letter_C8(global_data)

def generate_policy_number_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13410-generate_policy_number
    """
    global_data.WS_DATE_PART = date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d") #FUNCTION current_date
    global_data.WS_TYPE_PART = global_data.WS_POLICY_TYPE
    global_data.WS_RANDOM_PART = Decimal(random.random() * 99999) #FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    global_data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER = f"{global_data.WS_TYPE_PART}{global_data.WS_DATE_PART}{int(global_data.WS_RANDOM_PART)}"

def create_policy_record_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13420-create_policy_record
    """COBOL logic"""
    13430-set_beneficiaries
    """
    for global_data.WS_BENEF_IDX in range(1, 6): #PERFORM VARYING ws_benef_idx FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL ws_benef_idx > 5
        if global_data.BENEF_NAME[global_data.WS_BENEF_IDX] != "": #IF benef_name(ws_benef_idx) NOT  = None  # TODO: was SPACES
            ws_beneficiary_rec = BeneficiaryRecord() #INITIALIZE ws_beneficiary_rec
            ws_beneficiary_rec.BENEF_REC_POLICY = global_data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER #MOVE ws_policy_number TO benef_rec_policy
            ws_beneficiary_rec.BENEF_REC_NAME = global_data.BENEF_NAME[global_data.WS_BENEF_IDX] #MOVE benef_name(ws_benef_idx) TO benef_rec_name
            ws_beneficiary_rec.BENEF_REC_RELATION = global_data.BENEF_RELATION[global_data.WS_BENEF_IDX] #MOVE benef_relation(ws_benef_idx) TO benef_rec_relation
            ws_beneficiary_rec.BENEF_REC_PCT = global_data.BENEF_PCT[global_data.WS_BENEF_IDX] #MOVE benef_pct(ws_benef_idx) TO benef_rec_pct
            #WRITE beneficiary_record FROM ws_beneficiary_rec

def send_policy_docs_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13440-send_policy_docs
    """
    global_data.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'policy_issue' #MOVE 'policy_issue' TO ws_notif_type
    global_data.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL' #MOVE 'MAIL' TO ws_notif_channel
    global_data.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f"Your policy {global_data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER} has been issued"
    global_data.WS_NOTIF_BODY = "" #STRING 'Your policy ' DELIMITED SIZE ws_policy_number DELIMITED SIZE ' has been issued' DELIMITED SIZE INTO ws_notif_subject
    send_notification_C8(global_data) #PERFORM 15000-send_notification
def send_decline_letter_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13450-send_decline_letter
    """
    global_data.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'policy_decline'
    global_data.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'MAIL'
    global_data.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Regarding your insurance application'
    send_notification_C8(global_data)

def claims_handling_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13500-claims_handling
    """
    receive_claim_C8(global_data)
    validate_claim_C8(global_data)
    investigate_claim_C8(global_data)
    adjudicate_claim_C8(global_data)
    process_payment_C8(global_data)

def receive_claim_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13510-receive_claim
    """
    global_data.WS_CLAIM_DATE = date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d") #MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO ws_claim_date
    generate_claim_number_C8(global_data) #PERFORM 13515-generate_claim_number
    global_data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'RECEIVED' #MOVE 'RECEIVED' TO ws_claim_status

def generate_claim_number_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13515-generate_claim_number
    """
    global_data.WS_DATE_PART = date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d") #MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO ws_date_part
    global_data.WS_RANDOM_PART = Decimal(random.random() * 99999) #COMPUTE ws_random_part = FUNCTION RANDOM * 99999
    global_data.WS_CLAIM_NUMBER = f"CLM{global_data.WS_DATE_PART}{int(global_data.WS_RANDOM_PART)}" #STRING 'CLM' DELIMITED SIZE ws_date_part DELIMITED SIZE ws_random_part DELIMITED SIZE INTO ws_claim_number
def validate_claim_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13520-validate_claim
    """
    check_policy_status_C8(global_data)
    check_coverage_C8(global_data)
    check_deductible_C8(global_data)

def check_policy_status_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13522-check_policy_status
    """
    if global_data.WS_POLICY_STATUS != 'A':
        global_data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED'
        global_data.WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE'

def check_coverage_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13524-check_coverage
    """
    if global_data.WS_CLAIM_TYPE != global_data.WS_COVERED_PERILS:
        global_data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED'
        global_data.WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'NOT COVERED PERIL'

def check_deductible_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13526-check_deductible
    """
    if global_data.WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT <= global_data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE:
        global_data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED'
        global_data.WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE'

def investigate_claim_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13530-investigate_claim
    """
    if global_data.WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT > 10000:
        global_data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'INVESTIGATION'
        assign_adjuster_C8(global_data)
    fraud_check_C8(global_data)

def assign_adjuster_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13535-assign_adjuster
    """
    global_data.WS_ADJUSTER_ID = 'ADJ001'
    global_data.WS_NOTES = 'Assigned for investigation'

def fraud_check_C8(global_data: GlobalData) -> None:
    """
    13536-fraud_check
    """
    if global_data.WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 2:
        global_data.WS_FRAUD_REVIEW = 'Y'
    if global_data.WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT > global_data.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.8"):
        global_data.WS_FRA

def check_pep_status(pep_match_score: int):
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_PEP_STATUS, WS_PEP_SCORE, PEP_MATCH_SCORE
    WS_PEP_STATUS = 'Y'
    WS_PEP_SCORE = pep_match_score

def check_adverse_media_C9(customer_name: str) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CUSTOMER_NAME, MEDIA_REQUEST, MEDIA_RESPONSE, MEDIA_HITS_FOUND, WS_WATCHLIST_HITS
    WS_CUSTOMER_NAME = customer_name
    MEDIASRCH(MEDIA_REQUEST, MEDIA_RESPONSE)
    if MEDIA_HITS_FOUND > 0:
        WS_WATCHLIST_HITS += None  # TODO: was MEDIA_HITS_FOUND

def calculate_match_score_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_OFAC_SCORE, WS_MATCH_SCORE, WS_PEP_SCORE, WS_WATCHLIST_HITS
    if WS_OFAC_SCORE > 0:
        WS_MATCH_SCORE += None  # TODO: was WS_OFAC_SCORE
    if WS_PEP_SCORE > 0:
        WS_MATCH_SCORE += None  # TODO: was WS_PEP_SCORE
    if WS_WATCHLIST_HITS > 0:
      WS_MATCH_SCORE = WS_MATCH_SCORE / WS_WATCHLIST_HITS
    else:
        WS_MATCH_SCORE = 0

def determine_disposition_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_MATCH_SCORE, WS_MATCH_TYPE, WS_SAR_REQUIRED, WS_CASE_STATUS
    match True:
        case WS_MATCH_SCORE >= 90:
            WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'CONFIRMED'
            WS_SAR_REQUIRED = 'Y'
        case WS_MATCH_SCORE >= 75:
            WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'POTENTIAL'
            WS_CASE_STATUS = 'REVIEW'
        case WS_MATCH_SCORE >= 50:
            WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'WEAK'
            WS_CASE_STATUS = 'CLEARED'
        case _:
            WS_MATCH_TYPE = 'FALSE POSITIVE'
            WS_CASE_STATUS = 'CLEARED'

def kyc_verification_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    verify_identity_C9()
    verify_address_C9()
    verify_documents_C9()
    determine_kyc_status_C9()

def verify_identity_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CUSTOMER_SSN, WS_CUSTOMER_DOB, WS_CUSTOMER_NAME, ID_REQUEST, ID_RESPONSE, ID_VERIFIED, WS_ID_STATUS
    ID_REQUEST.ssn = WS_CUSTOMER_SSN  # Assuming ssn is a field in IDRequest
    ID_REQUEST.dob = WS_CUSTOMER_DOB  # Assuming dob is a field in IDRequest
    ID_REQUEST.name = WS_CUSTOMER_NAME # Assuming name is a field in IDRequest
    IDVERIFY(ID_REQUEST, ID_RESPONSE)
    if ID_VERIFIED == 'Y':
        WS_ID_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        WS_ID_STATUS = 'FAILED'

def verify_address_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CUSTOMER_ADDRESS, ADDR_REQUEST, ADDR_RESPONSE, ADDR_VERIFIED, WS_ADDR_STATUS
    ADDR_REQUEST.address = WS_CUSTOMER_ADDRESS # Assuming address is a field in AddrRequest
    ADDRVERIFY(ADDR_REQUEST, ADDR_RESPONSE)
    if ADDR_VERIFIED == 'Y':
        WS_ADDR_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        WS_ADDR_STATUS = 'UNVERIFIED'

def verify_documents_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_DOC_TYPE
    if WS_DOC_TYPE == 'PASSPORT':
        verify_passport_C9()
    elif WS_DOC_TYPE == 'LICENSE':
        verify_license_C9()
    else:
        verify_other_doc_C9()

def verify_passport_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_PASSPORT_NUMBER, WS_PASSPORT_COUNTRY, PASSPORT_REQ, PASSPORT_RESP, PASSPORT_VALID, WS_DOC_STATUS
    PASSPORT_REQ.passport_number = WS_PASSPORT_NUMBER # Assuming passport_number is a field in PassportReq
    PASSPORT_REQ.passport_country = WS_PASSPORT_COUNTRY # Assuming passport_country is a field in PassportReq
    PASSVERIFY(PASSPORT_REQ, PASSPORT_RESP)
    if PASSPORT_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_DOC_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        WS_DOC_STATUS = 'INVALID'

def verify_license_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_LICENSE_NUMBER, WS_LICENSE_STATE, LICENSE_REQ, LICENSE_RESP, LICENSE_VALID, WS_DOC_STATUS
    LICENSE_REQ.license_number = WS_LICENSE_NUMBER  # Assuming license_number is a field in LicenseReq
    LICENSE_REQ.license_state = WS_LICENSE_STATE # Assuming license_state is a field in LicenseReq
    LICVERIFY(LICENSE_REQ, LICENSE_RESP)
    if LICENSE_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_DOC_STATUS = 'VERIFIED'
    else:
        WS_DOC_STATUS = 'INVALID'

def verify_other_doc_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_DOC_STATUS
    WS_DOC_STATUS = 'MANUAL REVIEW'

def determine_kyc_status_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_ID_STATUS, WS_ADDR_STATUS, WS_DOC_STATUS, WS_KYC_STATUS
    if WS_ID_STATUS == 'VERIFIED' and WS_ADDR_STATUS == 'VERIFIED' and WS_DOC_STATUS == 'VERIFIED':
        WS_KYC_STATUS = 'APPROVED'
    else:
        WS_KYC_STATUS = 'PENDING'

        freeze_account_C9()

def escalate_to_compliance_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_ESCALATION_RECORD, ESC_REASON, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, ESC_DATE, ESC_PRIORITY
    WS_ESCALATION_RECORD = EscalationRecord()
    ESC_REASON = 'SANCTIONS HIT'
    WS_ESCALATION_RECORD.customer_id = WS_CUSTOMER_ID  # Assuming customer_id is a field in EscalationRecord
    WS_ESCALATION_RECORD.date = datetime.now()  # Assuming date is a field in EscalationRecord
    WS_ESCALATION_RECORD.priority = 'URGENT'  # Assuming priority is a field in EscalationRecord
    # Assuming "WRITE escalation_record FROM ws_escalation_record." writes the record to a file/database
    write_escalation_record(WS_ESCALATION_RECORD)

    WS_ACCOUNT_STATUS = 'F'

def transaction_monitoring_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    check_velocity_C9()
    check_patterns_C9()
    check_high_risk_C9()
    calculate_risk_score_C9()

def check_velocity_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_DAILY_TRANS_COUNT, WS_VELOCITY_THRESHOLD, WS_VELOCITY_FLAG, WS_FRAUD_SCORE, WS_DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_AMOUNT_THRESHOLD, WS_AMOUNT_FLAG
    if WS_DAILY_TRANS_COUNT > WS_VELOCITY_THRESHOLD:
        WS_VELOCITY_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 20
    if WS_DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT > WS_AMOUNT_THRESHOLD:
        WS_AMOUNT_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 20

def check_patterns_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_ROUND_AMOUNT_COUNT, WS_PATTERN_FLAG, WS_FRAUD_SCORE, WS_STRUCTURING_DETECTED
    if WS_ROUND_AMOUNT_COUNT > 5:
        WS_PATTERN_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 15
    if WS_STRUCTURING_DETECTED == 'Y':
        WS_PATTERN_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 30

def check_high_risk_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_HIGH_RISK_COUNTRY, WS_LOCATION_FLAG, WS_FRAUD_SCORE, WS_NEW_DEVICE, WS_DEVICE_FLAG
    if WS_HIGH_RISK_COUNTRY == 'Y':
        WS_LOCATION_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 25
    if WS_NEW_DEVICE == 'Y':
        WS_DEVICE_FLAG = 'Y'
        WS_FRAUD_SCORE += 10

def calculate_risk_score_C9() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_FRAUD_SCORE, WS_FRAUD_DECISION, WS_MANUAL_REVIEW
    match True:
        case WS_FRAUD_SCORE >= 80:
            WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'BLOCK'
            WS_MANUAL_REVIEW = 'Y'
        case WS_FRAUD_SCORE >= 60:
            WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'REVIEW'
            WS_MANUAL_REVIEW = 'Y'
        case WS_FRAUD_SCORE >= 40:
            WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'MONITOR'
        case _:
            WS_FRAUD_DECISION = 'APPROVE'

    SAR_SUBJECT_ADDR  = None  # TODO: was WS_CUSTOMER_ADDRESS

    global WS_CASE_ID, WS_DATE_PART, WS_RANDOM_PART
    WS_DATE_PART = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    WS_RANDOM_PART = int(random.random() * 99999)
    WS_CASE_ID = f"CS{WS_DATE_PART}{WS_RANDOM_PART}"

# Assuming these external calls/file reads are implemented elsewhere
def FUNCTION_INTEGER_OF_DATE(date_value: str) -> int:
    """Dummy implementation of integer_of_date"""
    return int(date_value.replace('-', ''))

def PINVERIFY(card_number: str, current_pin: str) -> str:
    """Dummy implementation of PINVERIFY"""
    return "MATCH" if current_pin == "1234" else "NOMATCH"

def PINENCRYPT(new_pin: str) -> str:
    """Dummy implementation of PINENCRYPT"""
    return f"Encrypted_{new_pin}"

WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE: int = 0

WS_LAST_RUN_DATE: str = "2024-01-01"
WS_RUN_FREQUENCY: str = "DAILY"

def evaluate_next_run_date_C10(ws_last_run_date: str, ws_run_frequency: str) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE
    match ws_run_frequency:
        case 'DAILY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = FUNCTION_INTEGER_OF_DATE(ws_last_run_date) + 1
        case 'WEEKLY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = FUNCTION_INTEGER_OF_DATE(ws_last_run_date) + 7
        case 'MONTHLY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = FUNCTION_INTEGER_OF_DATE(ws_last_run_date) + 30
        case 'QUARTERLY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = FUNCTION_INTEGER_OF_DATE(ws_last_run_date) + 90
        case 'YEARLY':
            WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE = FUNCTION_INTEGER_OF_DATE(ws_last_run_date) + 365

# Example usage:
# evaluate_next_run_date(WS_LAST_RUN_DATE, WS_RUN_FREQUENCY)
# print(f"Next Run Date: {WS_NEXT_RUN_DATE}")

# Data Analytics and Reporting Procedures
def data_analytics_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    collect_metrics_C10()
    aggregate_data_C10()
    calculate_kpi_C10()
    generate_dashboard_C10()
    export_data_C10()

def collect_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    collect_transaction_metrics_C10()
    collect_customer_metrics_C10()
    collect_performance_metrics_C10()

def collect_transaction_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT, WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT = 0
    WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        transaction_record = read_transaction_file()
        if transaction_record is None:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT += 1
            WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT += Decimal(str(transaction_record.get('trans_amount', 0))) # Assuming trans_amount is in the dict
    if WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT > 0:
        WS_AVG_TRANS_AMOUNT = WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT / WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def collect_customer_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS, WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS, WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS, WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS = 0
    WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS = 0
    WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        customer_record = read_customer_file()
        if customer_record is None:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            if customer_record.get('cust_status') == 'A':
                WS_ACTIVE_CUSTOMERS += 1
            if customer_record.get('cust_open_date', "") >= WS_PERIOD_START:
                WS_NEW_CUSTOMERS += 1
            if customer_record.get('cust_close_date', "") >= WS_PERIOD_START:
                WS_CHURNED_CUSTOMERS += 1
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def collect_performance_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL, WS_RESPONSE_COUNT, WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME, WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_RESPONSE_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        perf_record = read_perf_log_file()
        if perf_record is None:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL += Decimal(str(perf_record.get('perf_response_time', 0)))
            WS_RESPONSE_COUNT += 1
    if WS_RESPONSE_COUNT > 0:
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE_TIME = WS_RESPONSE_TIME_TOTAL / WS_RESPONSE_COUNT
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def aggregate_data_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    daily_aggregation_C10()
    weekly_aggregation_C10()
    monthly_aggregation_C10()

def daily_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_DAILY_SUMMARY
    WS_DAILY_SUMMARY = WsDailySummary()  # Initialize
    WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.daily_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.daily_trans_count = WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT
    WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.daily_trans_amount = WS_TOTAL_TRANS_AMOUNT
    # Assuming ws_total_deposits and ws_total_withdrawals are defined elsewhere
    WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.daily_deposits = Decimal('0.00') #WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS
    WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.daily_withdrawals = Decimal('0.00') #WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS
    write_daily_summary_record(WS_DAILY_SUMMARY)

def weekly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY
    if WS_DAY_OF_WEEK == 7:
        WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY = WsWeeklySummary() # Initialize
        WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.weekly_week  = None  # TODO: was WS_WEEK_NUMBER
        sum_week_data_C10()
        write_weekly_summary_record(WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY)

def sum_week_data_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY
    WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.weekly_trans_count = 0
    WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.weekly_trans_amount = Decimal('0.00')
    for _ in range(7):
        # Assuming daily_trans_count and daily_trans_amount can be read
        WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.weekly_trans_count += WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.daily_trans_count #DAILY_TRANS_COUNT
        WS_WEEKLY_SUMMARY.weekly_trans_amount += WS_DAILY_SUMMARY.daily_trans_amount #DAILY_TRANS_AMOUNT

def monthly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY
    if WS_END_OF_MONTH == 'Y':
        WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY = WsMonthlySummary()  # Initialize
        WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_month  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURR_MONTH
        WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_year  = None  # TODO: was WS_CURR_YEAR
        sum_month_data_C10()
        write_monthly_summary_record(WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY)

def sum_month_data_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY, WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_trans_count = 0
    WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_trans_amount = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_new_accounts = 0
    WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_closed_accounts = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        daily_summary_record = read_daily_summary_file()
        if daily_summary_record is None:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            if daily_summary_record.get('daily_month') == WS_CURR_MONTH:
                WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_trans_count += daily_summary_record.get('daily_trans_count', 0)
                WS_MONTHLY_SUMMARY.monthly_trans_amount += Decimal(str(daily_summary_record.get('daily_trans_amount', 0)))
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def calculate_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    calc_financial_kpi_C10()
    calc_operational_kpi_C10()
    calc_customer_kpi_C10()

def calc_financial_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_ROA, WS_ROE, WS_NIM
    if WS_TOTAL_ASSETS > 0:
        WS_ROA = (WS_NET_INCOME / WS_TOTAL_ASSETS) * 100
    if WS_TOTAL_EQUITY > 0:
        WS_ROE = (WS_NET_INCOME / WS_TOTAL_EQUITY) * 100
    if WS_INTEREST_EXPENSE > 0:
        WS_NIM = ((WS_INTEREST_INCOME - WS_INTEREST_EXPENSE) / WS_EARNING_ASSETS) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_ERROR_RATE, WS_SLA_COMPLIANCE, WS_FIRST_CALL_RESOLUTION
    if WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT > 0:
        WS_ERROR_RATE = (WS_ERROR_COUNT / WS_TOTAL_TRANS_COUNT) * 100
    WS_SLA_COMPLIANCE = (WS_WITHIN_SLA_COUNT / WS_TOTAL_CASES) * 100
    WS_FIRST_CALL_RESOLUTION = (WS_FCR_COUNT / WS_TOTAL_CALLS) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""COBOL: shipment_record"""
    pass

def shipping_logic() -> None:
    """COBOL: Lines not in a named paragraph"""
    global SHIP_METHOD, SHIP_EST_DELIVERY, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    
    if True: # The COBOL code implies this is the "TRUE" branch
        SHIP_METHOD = 'EXPRESS'  # MOVE 'EXPRESS' TO ship_method
        SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = int(WS_PROCESS_DATE.toordinal()) + 2  # COMPUTE ship_est_delivery = FUNCTION integer_of_date(ws_process_date) + 2
    else:
        SHIP_METHOD = 'STANDARD'  # MOVE 'STANDARD' TO ship_method
        SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = int(WS_PROCESS_DATE.toordinal()) + 7  # COMPUTE ship_est_delivery = FUNCTION integer_of_date(ws_process_date) + 7

    # WRITE shipment_record FROM ws_shipment_record.  (Assuming writing to file/database is handled elsewhere)
    print("Writing shipment record (simulated)")

def validate_stop_request_C12(ws_check_number: int, ws_check_already_cleared: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """29100-validate_stop_request
    """
    ws_stop_valid = 'Y'
    ws_stop_reject = ""
    if ws_check_number == 0:
        ws_stop_valid = 'N'
        ws_stop_reject = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
    if ws_check_already_cleared == 'Y':
        ws_stop_valid = 'N'
        ws_stop_reject = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'
    return ws_stop_valid, ws_stop_reject

    """
    ws_stop_record = WsStopRecordC12()

    stop_check_number = ws_check_number
    stop_amount = ws_check_amount
    stop_payee = ws_payee_name
    stop_effective_date = ws_process_date
    stop_expiry_date = ws_process_date + 180  #Assuming date is in YYYYMMDD format
    stop_status = 'A'
    ws_stop_record.stop_account = stop_account
    ws_stop_record.stop_check_number = stop_check_number
    ws_stop_record.stop_amount = stop_amount
    ws_stop_record.stop_payee = stop_payee
    ws_stop_record.stop_effective_date = stop_effective_date
    ws_stop_record.stop_expiry_date = stop_expiry_date
    ws_stop_record.stop_status = stop_status
    # WRITE stop_record FROM ws_stop_record. (Assuming you have a write function)
    return ws_stop_record

def check_cvv_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_auth_cvv: str) -> str:
    """31117-check_cvv
    """
    ws_cvv_result = cvvverify(ws_auth_card_number, ws_auth_cvv) #CALL 'CVVVERIFY' USING ws_auth_card_number ws_auth_cvv ws_cvv_result
    if ws_cvv_result == 'M':
        ws_cvv_valid = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_cvv_valid = 'N'
    return ws_cvv_valid

def check_fraud_score_C12(ws_auth_request: str) -> str:
    """31120-check_fraud_score
    """
    fraud_response = fraudcheck(ws_auth_request) #CALL 'FRAUDCHECK' USING ws_auth_request ws_fraud_response
    fraud_score = fraud_response["fraud_score"]
    fraud_decline_code = fraud_response["fraud_decline_code"]
    if fraud_score < 70:
        ws_fraud_approved = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_fraud_approved = 'N'
        ws_auth_decline_code = fraud_decline_code #MOVE fraud_decline_code TO ws_auth_decline_code
    return ws_fraud_approved

def check_available_credit_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_auth_amount: Decimal, card_account_file: dict) -> str:
    """31130-check_available_credit
    """
    ws_search_key = ws_auth_card_number
    ws_card_account_rec = card_account_file.get(ws_search_key) #READ card_account_file INTO ws_card_account_rec (simulated)
    if ws_card_account_rec is None:
        ws_credit_available = 'N'
        ws_auth_decline_code = '51'
    elif ws_card_account_rec["available_credit"] >= ws_auth_amount:
        ws_credit_available = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_credit_available = 'N'
        ws_auth_decline_code = '51' #MOVE '51' TO ws_auth_decline_code
    return ws_credit_available

def approve_auth_C12(ws_auth_amount: Decimal, available_credit: Decimal, generate_auth_code: callable, record_authorization: callable) -> None:
    """31140-approve_auth
    """COBOL logic"""31145-generate_auth_code
    """
    import random
    ws_auth_code = int(random.random() * 999999) #FUNCTION RANDOM * 999999
    ws_auth_response_auth_code = ws_auth_code
    return ws_auth_response_auth_code

    auth_rec_merchant = ws_merchant_id
    auth_rec_status = 'P'
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_card = auth_rec_card
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_amount = auth_rec_amount
    ws_auth_record.auth_rec_code = auth_rec_code

    #WRITE auth_record FROM ws_auth_record

def decline_auth_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_auth_amount: Decimal, ws_auth_decline_code: str) -> WsDeclineRecordC12:
    """31150-decline_auth
    """
    ws_auth_response_code = ws_auth_decline_code
    ws_decline_record = WsDeclineRecordC12()
    decline_rec_card = ws_auth_card_number
    decline_rec_amount = ws_auth_amount
    decline_rec_code = ws_auth_decline_code
    decline_rec_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_card = decline_rec_card
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_amount = decline_rec_amount
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_code = decline_rec_code
    ws_decline_record.decline_rec_date = decline_rec_date
    return ws_decline_record
    #WRITE decline_record FROM ws_decline_record

def capture_transaction_C12(ws_capture_request: str, validate_auth_code: callable, create_capture_record: callable) -> None:
    """31200-capture_transaction
    """COBOL logic"""31210-validate_auth_code
    """
    ws_auth_valid = 'N'
    auth_search_key = ws_capture_auth_code
    ws_auth_rec = auth_file.get(auth_search_key) #READ auth_file INTO ws_auth_rec, KEY IS auth_code (simulated)
    if ws_auth_rec is None: #INVALID KEY
        ws_auth_valid = 'N'
    else: #NOT INVALID KEY
        if ws_auth_rec["auth_rec_status"] == 'P':
            ws_auth_valid = 'Y'
    return ws_auth_valid

def create_capture_record_C12(ws_capture_amount: Decimal, ws_capture_auth_code: int, ws_auth_rec: dict) -> WsCaptureRecordC12:
    """31220-create_capture_record
    """
    ws_auth_rec["auth_rec_status"] = 'C' #MOVE 'C' TO auth_rec_status
    #REWRITE auth_record FROM ws_auth_rec (Assuming you have a rewrite function)
    ws_capture_record = WsCaptureRecordC12()
    capture_card = ws_auth_rec["auth_rec_card"]
    capture_amount = ws_capture_amount
    capture_auth_code = ws_capture_auth_code
    capture_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    ws_capture_record.capture_card = capture_card
    ws_capture_record.capture_amount = capture_amount
    ws_capture_record.capture_auth_code = capture_auth_code
    ws_capture_record.capture_date = capture_date
    return ws_capture_record
    #WRITE capture_record FROM ws_capture_record

def process_settlement_C12(batch_transactions: callable, calculate_fees: callable, create_funding_record: callable, send_settlement_file: callable) -> None:
    """31300-process_settlement
    """COBOL logic"""31310-batch_transactions
    """
    ws_batch_total = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_batch_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    for ws_capture_rec in capture_file:
        if ws_capture_rec is None: #AT END
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else: #NOT AT END
            if ws_capture_rec["capture_settled"] == 'N':
                ws_batch_total += ws_capture_rec["capture_amount"]
                ws_batch_count += 1
                ws_capture_rec["capture_settled"] = 'Y' #MOVE 'Y' TO capture_settled
                #REWRITE capture_record FROM ws_capture_rec (Assuming you have a rewrite function)

    ws_eof_flag = 'N' #MOVE 'N' TO ws_eof_flag
    return ws_batch_total, ws_batch_count

def calculate_fees_C12(ws_batch_total: Decimal, ws_batch_count: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """31320-calculate_fees
    """
    ws_interchange_fee = ws_batch_total * Decimal("0.0175") #COMPUTE ws_interchange_fee = ws_batch_total * 0.0175
    ws_assessment_fee = ws_batch_total * Decimal("0.0015") #COMPUTE ws_assessment_fee = ws_batch_total * 0.0015
    ws_processor_fee = Decimal(ws_batch_count * 0.10) #COMPUTE ws_processor_fee = ws_batch_count * 0.10
    ws_total_fees = ws_interchange_fee + ws_assessment_fee + ws_processor_fee #COMPUTE ws_total_fees = ws_interchange_fee + ws_assessment_fee + ws_processor_fee
    return ws_interchange_fee, ws_assessment_fee, ws_processor_fee, ws_total_fees

    """
    ws_net_funding = ws_

# 34100-calculate_capital_ratios
def calculate_capital_ratios_34100_C14(calculate_tier1_34110_C14: callable, calculate_tier2_34120_C14: callable, calculate_ratios_34130_C14: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""

# 34110-calculate_tier1
def calculate_tier1_34110_C14(ws_common_stock: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings: Decimal, ws_aoci: Decimal, ws_goodwill: Decimal, ws_intangibles: Decimal, ws_dta_deduction: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_tier1_capital: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_common_stock
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_retained_earnings
    ws_tier1_capital += ws_aoci
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_goodwill
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_intangibles
    ws_tier1_capital -= ws_dta_deduction
    return ws_tier1_capital

# 34120-calculate_tier2
def calculate_tier2_34120_C14(ws_sub_debt: Decimal, ws_alll_eligible: Decimal, ws_tier1_capital: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_tier2_capital: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_tier2_capital += ws_sub_debt
    ws_tier2_capital += ws_alll_eligible
    ws_total_capital: Decimal = ws_tier1_capital + ws_tier2_capital
    return ws_tier2_capital, ws_total_capital

# 34130-calculate_ratios
def calculate_ratios_34130_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_tier1_capital: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal, ws_total_assets: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_cet1_ratio: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_capital_ratio: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_leverage_ratio: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

    if ws_risk_weighted_assets > 0:
        ws_cet1_ratio = (ws_tier1_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
        ws_capital_ratio = (ws_total_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
    
    if ws_total_assets > 0:
        ws_leverage_ratio = (ws_tier1_capital / ws_total_assets) * 100

    return ws_cet1_ratio, ws_capital_ratio, ws_leverage_ratio

# 34200-risk_weighted_assets
def risk_weighted_assets_34200_C14(credit_rwa_34210_C14: callable, market_rwa_34220_C14: callable, operational_rwa_34230_C14: callable) -> Decimal:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_risk_weighted_assets = credit_rwa_34210_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets)
    ws_risk_weighted_assets = market_rwa_34220_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets)
    ws_risk_weighted_assets = operational_rwa_34230_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets)
    return ws_risk_weighted_assets

# 34210-credit_rwa
def credit_rwa_34210_C14(ws_cash_position: Decimal, ws_govt_securities: Decimal, ws_bank_deposits: Decimal, ws_residential_mortgages: Decimal, ws_commercial_loans: Decimal, ws_consumer_loans: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_cash_rwa: Decimal = ws_cash_position * Decimal("0.00")
    ws_govt_rwa: Decimal = ws_govt_securities * Decimal("0.00")
    ws_bank_rwa: Decimal = ws_bank_deposits * Decimal("0.20")
    ws_mortgage_rwa: Decimal = ws_residential_mortgages * Decimal("0.50")
    ws_commercial_rwa: Decimal = ws_commercial_loans * Decimal("1.00")
    ws_consumer_rwa: Decimal = ws_consumer_loans * Decimal("1.00")

    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_cash_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_govt_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_bank_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_mortgage_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_commercial_rwa
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_consumer_rwa
    return ws_risk_weighted_assets

# 34220-market_rwa
def market_rwa_34220_C14(ws_trading_assets: Decimal, ws_market_risk_factor: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_market_rwa: Decimal = ws_trading_assets * ws_market_risk_factor
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_market_rwa
    return ws_risk_weighted_assets

# 34230-operational_rwa
def operational_rwa_34230_C14(ws_gross_income: Decimal, ws_operational_factor: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_operational_rwa: Decimal = ws_gross_income * ws_operational_factor * Decimal("12.5")
    ws_risk_weighted_assets += ws_operational_rwa
    return ws_risk_weighted_assets

# 34300-capital_planning
def capital_planning_34300_C14(project_capital_needs_34310_C14: callable, identify_capital_actions_34320_C14: callable, update_capital_plan_34330_C14: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    project_capital_needs_34310_C14()
    identify_capital_actions_34320_C14()
    update_capital_plan_34330_C14()

# 34310-project_capital_needs
def project_capital_needs_34310_C14(ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_growth_rate: Decimal, ws_target_ratio: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_projected_rwa: Decimal = ws_risk_weighted_assets * (1 + ws_growth_rate)
    ws_required_capital: Decimal = ws_projected_rwa * ws_target_ratio / 100
    ws_capital_gap: Decimal = ws_required_capital - ws_total_capital
    return ws_projected_rwa, ws_required_capital, ws_capital_gap

# 34320-identify_capital_actions
def identify_capital_actions_34320_C14(ws_capital_gap: Decimal, ws_retained_earnings_proj: Decimal, ws_sub_debt_capacity: Decimal) -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_capital_action: str = ""
    if ws_capital_gap > 0:
        if ws_capital_gap <= ws_retained_earnings_proj:
            ws_capital_action = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
        elif ws_capital_gap <= ws_sub_debt_capacity:
            ws_capital_action = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
        else:
            ws_capital_action = 'EQUITY RAISE'
    else:
        ws_capital_action = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'
    return ws_capital_action

# 34440-compile_results
def compile_results_34440_C14(ws_stress_pass_fail: str, remediation_actions_34460_C14: callable) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    print('STRESS TEST RESULTS COMPILED')
    if ws_stress_pass_fail == 'FAIL':
        remediation_actions_34460_C14()

# 34450-calculate_stress_impact
def calculate_stress_impact_34450_C14(ws_scenario_name: str, ws_rate_shock: Decimal, ws_gdp_change: Decimal, ws_unemployment_rate: Decimal, ws_housing_decline: Decimal, ws_loan_portfolio: Decimal, ws_stress_lgd: Decimal, ws_stress_pd: Decimal, ws_trading_assets: Decimal, ws_total_capital: Decimal, ws_risk_weighted_assets: Decimal, ws_min_capital_ratio: Decimal) -> str:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_credit_losses: Decimal = ws_loan_portfolio * ws_stress_lgd * ws_stress_pd
    ws_market_losses: Decimal = ws_trading_assets * ws_rate_shock / 100
    ws_stress_losses: Decimal = ws_credit_losses + ws_market_losses
    ws_stressed_capital: Decimal = ws_total_capital - ws_stress_losses
    ws_stressed_ratio: Decimal = (ws_stressed_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
    ws_stress_pass_fail: str = ""
    if ws_stressed_ratio >= ws_min_capital_ratio:
        ws_stress_pass_fail = 'PASS'
    else:
        ws_stress_pass_fail = 'FAIL'
    return ws_stress_pass_fail

    """COBOL logic"""

        post_to_accounts_35120_C14()
        record_posting_35130_C14()

# 35110-validate_journal_entry
def validate_journal_entry_35110_C14(je_debit: list[Decimal], je_credit: list[Decimal]) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """COBOL logic"""
    ws_je_valid: str = 'Y'
    ws_total_debits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_credits: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_je_error: str = ""

    for ws_je_idx in range(50):
        ws_total_debits += je_debit[ws_je_idx]
        ws_total_credits += je_credit[ws_je_idx]
    
    if ws_total_debits != ws_total_credits:
        ws_je_valid = 'N'
        ws_je_error = 'OUT OF BALANCE'
    
    return ws_je_valid, ws_je_error

def perform_until(condition, action: callable):
    """COBOL logic"""
    while not condition():
        action()

def perform_varying(variable: str, start: int, step: int, condition: callable, action: callable):
    """COBOL logic"""
    globals()[variable] = start
    while not condition():
        action()
        globals()[variable] += step

def load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL Paragraph: 37310-load_ic_balances."""
    global WS_IC_COUNT, WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_IC_ARRAY, INTERCOMPANY_FILE

    WS_IC_COUNT = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    WS_IC_ARRAY = []

    def read_ic_record():
        """Reads a record from the intercompany_file."""
        global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_IC_COUNT, WS_IC_ARRAY
        try:
            # Assuming INTERCOMPANY_FILE is a list of WsIcBalanceC15 objects for demonstration
            if WS_IC_COUNT < len(INTERCOMPANY_FILE):
                ws_ic_balance = INTERCOMPANY_FILE[WS_IC_COUNT] #Assuming INTERCOMPANY_FILE contains WS_IC_BALANCE Objects
                WS_IC_COUNT += 1
                WS_IC_ARRAY.append(ws_ic_balance)
            else:
                WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        except Exception as e:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            print(f"Error reading intercompany_file: {e}")

    perform_until(lambda: WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y', read_ic_record)
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL Paragraph: 37320-match_ic_pairs."""
    global WS_IC_IDX, WS_IC_COUNT

    def find_ic_counterpart_wrapper():
        find_ic_counterpart_C15()

    perform_varying()
        variable='WS_IC_IDX',
        start=1,
        step=1,
        condition=lambda: WS_IC_IDX > WS_IC_COUNT,
        action=find_ic_counterpart_wrapper
    )

def find_ic_counterpart_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL Paragraph: 37325-find_ic_counterpart."""COBOL logic"""COBOL Paragraph: 37326-log_ic_diff."""
    global WS_SEARCH_FROM, WS_SEARCH_TO, WS_IC_DIFF, IC_DIFF_RECORD

    ws_ic_diff_rec: WsIcDiffRecC15 = WsIcDiffRecC15()
    ws_ic_diff_rec.icd_from  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_FROM
    ws_ic_diff_rec.icd_to  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_TO
    ws_ic_diff_rec.icd_amount = Decimal(str(WS_IC_DIFF))

    # Assuming IC_DIFF_RECORD is a file object opened for writing
    # IC_DIFF_RECORD.write(str(ws_ic_diff_rec))
    print(f"Writing to IC_DIFF_RECORD: {ws_ic_diff_rec}")  # For demonstration purposes

def move_to_archive_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL Paragraph: 38410-move_to_archive."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, WS_ARCHIVE_DATE, AUDIT_FILE, ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

    def read_audit_record():
        """Reads a record from the audit_file."""
        global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP, WS_ARCHIVE_DATE, AUDIT_FILE, ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD
        try:
            # Assuming AUDIT_FILE is a list of WsAuditRecordC15 objects for demonstration
            if hasattr(AUDIT_FILE, '__len__') and len(AUDIT_FILE) > 0:
                ws_audit_record = AUDIT_FILE.pop(0) # Assuming AUDIT_FILE contains WS_AUDIT_RECORD objects
                if ws_audit_record.ws_audit_timestamp < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:
                   #Assuming ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD is a file object for writing
                   #ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD.write(ws_audit_record)
                   print(f"Writing to ARCHIVE_AUDIT_RECORD: {ws_audit_record}")
                   print("Deleting from AUDIT_FILE (simulated)")
            else:
                WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        except Exception as e:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
            print(f"Error reading audit_file: {e}")

    perform_until(lambda: WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y', read_audit_record)
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

import subprocess

def cpu_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL Paragraph: 39110-cpu_metrics."""
    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATION, WS_CPU_ALERT
    # Simulate calling an external program 'GETCPU'
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(['GETCPU'], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
        WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = int(result.stdout.strip())  # Assuming GETCPU returns an integer
    except FileNotFoundError:
        print("Error: 'GETCPU' program not found.  Using dummy value for CPU Utilization.")
        WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = 75 # Dummy value for example
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error calling 'GETCPU': {e}. Using dummy value for CPU Utilization.")
        WS_CPU_UTILIZATION = 75 # Dummy value for example

    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATION > 80:
        WS_CPU_ALERT = 'Y'

    WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD = 2.0
    WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD = 100

def performance_loop_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL Paragraph: 32000-performance_loop."""
    global WS_TRANS_COUNT, WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS, WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME

    # Simulate a loop that collects performance data
    for _ in range(3):  # Simulate 3 iterations
        start_time = 0  # Time.time() # Removed time dependency
        WS_TRANS_COUNT += 10
        WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS += 1
        WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME += 15  # Simulate response time

        collect_metrics_C15()

def transaction_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL Paragraph: 39140-transaction_metrics."""
    global WS_TPS, WS_AVG_RESPONSE

    if WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS != 0:
        WS_TPS = WS_TRANS_COUNT / WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS
    else:
        WS_TPS = 0

    if WS_TRANS_COUNT != 0:
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE = WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME / WS_TRANS_COUNT
    else:
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE = 0

def analyze_performance_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL Paragraph: 39200-analyze_performance."""
    global WS_AVG_RESPONSE, WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD, WS_PERF_DEGRADED, \
           WS_TPS, WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD, WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW

    if WS_AVG_RESPONSE > WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD:
        WS_PERF_DEGRADED = 'Y'

    if WS_TPS < WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD:
        WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW = 'Y'

def generate_alerts_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL Paragraph: 39300-generate_alerts."""
    if WS_CPU_ALERT == 'Y':
        send_cpu_alert_C15()

    if WS_MEMORY_ALERT == 'Y':
        send_memory_alert_C15()

    if WS_PERF_DEGRADED == 'Y':
        send_perf_alert_C15()

def send_cpu_alert_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL Paragraph: 39310-send_cpu_alert."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT, WS_CPU_UTILIZATION

    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'high_cpu'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f'ALERT: CPU utilization at {WS_CPU_UTILIZATION}%'
    send_notification_C15()

def send_memory_alert_C15() -> None:
    """COBOL Paragraph: 39320-send_memory_alert."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT

    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'high_memory'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'ALERT: High memory utilization'
    send_notification_C15()
